- [ ] **Step 3: Add the backstop, isolation and PII tests**

**REACHING THE BACKSTOP IS THE WHOLE POINT, and it is easy to get wrong.** `processRow` wraps SIX throw sites - `repo.claim`, `conversations.getById`, `contacts.getById`, `contacts.findByPhone`, `messages.listByConversation`, and all four `repo.complete` calls - so a test that makes any of those reject exercises the ordinary `failed()` path and **the backstop never executes**. Such a test cannot fail for the reason its name claims.

The sites `processRow` deliberately does NOT wrap:

| Unwrapped site | Why | Usable as a backstop trigger? |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `applyExtraction` | guards every side effect; no `apply` error kind (spec 6.3) | **Yes** - `apply.ts:443-445` calls `deps.events.emit` OUTSIDE any try, so a throwing emitter propagates |
| `driver.extract` | returns `ok:false` instead of throwing | Yes, but apply never runs, so it cannot demonstrate the orphan case |
| the pure builders (`toUtterances`, `capUtterances`, `buildFullRunWindow`, `buildDecisions`, `parseExtractionOps`) | pure, and `parseExtractionOps` is total | not injectable |

So the backstop tests below inject a throwing `applyEvents.emit`. That fires AFTER `applyExtraction` has mutated the contact and stamped `draft.runId` onto the suggestion row - exactly the state whose loss the caller-owned draft exists to prevent.

```ts
  it('the BACKSTOP genuinely executes and writes the SAME draft, never a fresh one', async () => {
    // apply.ts:443-445 emits OUTSIDE any try, and processRow deliberately does
    // not wrap applyExtraction - so this throw is UNWRAPPED and reaches
    // runDueExtractions' catch. Making repo.complete or a repo read throw would
    // NOT reach it: processRow wraps all six of those, and the test would
    // silently exercise the ordinary failed() path instead.
    const h = makeHarness({
      dueRows: [dueRow()],
      messages: [msg(10, 'inbound', 'EXTRACT:{"fields":{"pets":{"op":"suggest","value":"two cats"}}}')],
      contact: tenantContactWith({ pets: 'a dog' }),
      conversation: convWith('c1'),
    });
    h.applyEvents.emit.mockImplementationOnce(() => {
      throw new TypeError('unexpected defect in the event bus');
    });

    const out = await runDueExtractions(NOW, h.deps);

    expect(out).toEqual({ processed: 0, failed: 1 });
    expect(h.aiRuns.putRun).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    // PROOF the backstop ran rather than the ordinary failed() path: only the
    // backstop stamps 'repo' from an unhandled throw at this point, and
    // processRow never reached its own complete() call.
    expect(h.runs[0]!.error).toMatchObject({ kind: 'repo', message: /unexpected defect/ });
    expect(h.repo.complete).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    // THE ORPHAN GUARD: the suggestion apply just wrote carries this run's id,
    // so it stays stampable. A backstop that minted a fresh runId would fail here.
    const stamped = (h.repo.putSuggestion as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mock.calls[0]![0] as { runId?: string };
    expect(stamped.runId).toBeDefined();
    expect(h.runs[0]!.runId).toBe(stamped.runId);
    // And nothing built BEFORE the throw was lost. Decisions are built AFTER
    // applyExtraction returns, so they are legitimately absent here - the
    // window and the raw response are not.
    expect(h.runs[0]!.window!.detail).toBe('full');
    expect(h.runs[0]!.rawText).toContain('two cats');
    expect(h.runs[0]!.decisions).toEqual({});
    // The backstop must still route the row through backoff/park.
    expect(h.repo.fail).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('an UNWRAPPED throw on one row never aborts the rest of the batch', async () => {
    // The backstop's original purpose: per-row isolation against a defect
    // processRow does not catch.
    const h = makeHarness({
      dueRows: [dueRow({ conversationId: 'conv1' }), dueRow({ conversationId: 'conv2' })],
      messages: [msg(10, 'inbound', 'EXTRACT:{"fields":{"pets":{"op":"suggest","value":"two cats"}}}')],
      contact: tenantContactWith({ pets: 'a dog' }),
      conversation: convWith('c1'),
    });
    h.applyEvents.emit.mockImplementationOnce(() => {
      throw new TypeError('unexpected defect in the event bus');
    });

    const out = await runDueExtractions(NOW, h.deps);

    expect(out.failed).toBe(1);
    expect(out.processed).toBe(1); // the SECOND row completed normally
    expect(h.aiRuns.putRun).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2); // both rows recorded
  });

  it('a throwing run-log write leaves the extraction outcome, cursor and attempts untouched', async () => {
    // Observability that can break what it observes is worse than none.
    const h = makeHarness({
      dueRows: [dueRow()],
      messages: [msg(10, 'inbound', 'EXTRACT:{"fields":{"pets":{"op":"write","value":"two cats"}}}')],
      contact: tenantContact(),
      conversation: convWith('c1'),
      aiRuns: {
        putRun: vi.fn(async () => { throw new Error('ai_runs is on fire'); }),
        setVerdict: vi.fn(async () => true),
      },
    });

    const out = await runDueExtractions(NOW, h.deps);

    expect(out).toEqual({ processed: 1, failed: 0 });
    expect(h.repo.complete).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(h.repo.fail).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(h.contactsUpdate).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('PII GUARD: no run-path error object ever carries rawText or a decision value', async () => {
    // A returned draft never touches a logger. logger.error({ err }) is used
    // throughout this codebase and serializes an Error WHOLESALE (design 8).
    const capture = createLogCapture();
    const h = makeHarness({
      dueRows: [dueRow()],
      messages: [msg(10, 'inbound', 'EXTRACT:{"fields":{"pets":{"op":"write","value":"SECRETVALUE"}}}')],
      contact: tenantContact(),
      conversation: convWith('c1'),
      logger: createLogger({ destination: capture.stream, level: 'debug' }),
    });
    (h.repo.complete as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('complete boom'));

    await runDueExtractions(NOW, h.deps);

    const logged = capture.text();
    // GUARD THE GUARD: a broken capture would make both not.toContain
    // assertions pass vacuously. The complete() failure path logs at error, so
    // this is never legitimately empty.
    expect(logged.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(logged).toContain('extraction poll: row failed');
    expect(logged).not.toContain('SECRETVALUE');
    expect(logged).not.toContain('EXTRACT:');
    // And the RECORD does carry it - stored, never logged.
    expect(JSON.stringify(h.runs[0])).toContain('SECRETVALUE');
  });
```

# SLICE 11 - Verdict write-back on ALL THREE resolution surfaces

Spec 7.3 names three surfaces, and an independent grep of `app/src/` CONFIRMS the enumeration is complete: `deleteSuggestion` has exactly six call sites (all below), `putDismissal` exactly one (`app/src/routes/suggestions.ts:334`), and `putSuggestion` exactly one production path (`app/src/services/extraction/apply.ts:471`, via `putSuggestionSafe`, with five callers). `app/src/lib/seedData.ts` writes NO `sugg#` rows.

**Every mutation surface of the protected state, as explicit tasks.** Spec 7.3 (as amended) is emphatic that accept is not one call site but FOUR: instrumenting only the generic branch would silently miss `status`, `phone` and `address` accepts - three of the most common targets.

| Surface | Site | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- |
| accept - `status` | `app/src/routes/suggestions.ts:185` | `accepted` |
| accept - `phone` | `app/src/routes/suggestions.ts:235` | `accepted` |
| accept - `address` | `app/src/routes/suggestions.ts:270` | `accepted` |
| accept - the eight fields | `app/src/routes/suggestions.ts:304` | `accepted` |
| dismiss - ANY target incl. `type` | `app/src/routes/suggestions.ts:339` | `dismissed` |
| human PATCH edit | `app/src/routes/contacts.ts:1321-1327` | `superseded_by_human_edit`, except `type` -> `accepted` |
| a later run displacing an earlier one | the JOB, from `ApplyOutcome.displaced` | `superseded` |

**Target `type` is partly special** (spec 7.3): the ACCEPT route REFUSES it (`app/src/routes/suggestions.ts:159-162`, `accept_type_via_triage`), so `type` reaches `accepted` ONLY through the contacts PATCH. But the DISMISS route (`:316`) carries no target restriction and handles `type` today, so surface 1 still resolves it to `dismissed`. Instrumenting only the PATCH would strand dismissed `type` decisions.

**Other surfaces that TOUCH this state, enumerated as watch items (no code change):**
- Readers of `SuggestionItem`: `app/src/routes/suggestions.ts:147`, `:187`, `:237`, `:272`, `:306`, `:341` (`listSuggestionsByContact`) and `app/src/routes/today.ts` (`listPending`). They return whole items, so `runId` now rides to the client - harmless; the dashboard mirror gains an optional field in Task 23 Step 5.
- **The dev seam at `app/src/routes/dev.ts:487`** (`POST /__dev/voice/transcript-fixture`) calls `extraction.scheduleExtraction(conversationId, 'voice', nowIso)`, so a following tick MANUFACTURES a `voice`-trigger run and, if the fixture's transcript carries an `EXTRACT:` marker, a pending suggestion with a `runId`. No change is needed - it flows through the same job - but Task 30's e2e must expect `trigger: 'voice'` rows in the log from any spec that plants a transcript.
- `app/src/lib/devReset.ts:75` iterates `TABLES`, so the reseed wipes `ai_runs` automatically once Task 1 registers it. No change needed; confirm by reading, do not edit.

and to the construction block (`:115-139`): `const aiRuns = deps.aiRunsRepo ?? createAiRunsRepo({ logger: deps.logger });`

Add one helper below `serializeContact` (`:111-113`):

```ts
/**
 * Stamp the resolved verdict onto the run that produced this suggestion.
 * BEST-EFFORT on every path: a run-log hiccup must never fail the human's
 * action, and a `false` return simply means the run already TTLd (the
 * attribute_exists guard, design 7.3).
 */
async function stampVerdict(
  aiRuns: AiRunsRepo,
  log: Logger,
  suggestion: SuggestionItem,
  verdict: 'accepted' | 'dismissed',
  at: string,
  actor: string | undefined,
): Promise<void> {
  // Rows written before the run log existed carry no runId - nothing to stamp.
  if (suggestion.runId === undefined) return;
  if (!isDecisionTarget(suggestion.target)) return;
  try {
    await aiRuns.setVerdict(suggestion.runId, suggestion.target, verdict, {
      at,
      ...(actor !== undefined && { by: actor }),
    });
  } catch (err) {
    log.warn(
      { contactId: suggestion.ownerContactId, target: suggestion.target, err },
      'ai run verdict stamp failed (best-effort)',
    );
  }
}
```

Insert one call immediately AFTER each `deleteSuggestion` and BEFORE the `events.emit`:

- `:185` (status), `:235` (phone), `:270` (address), `:304` (fields): `await stampVerdict(aiRuns, log, suggestion, 'accepted', now, actor);`
- `:339` (dismiss): `await stampVerdict(aiRuns, log, suggestion, 'dismissed', now, actor);` - the dismiss handler has no `now` in scope, so add `const now = new Date().toISOString();` near `:320`.

Imports: `createAiRunsRepo, type AiRunsRepo` from `../repos/aiRunsRepo.js`; `type SuggestionItem` from `../repos/extractionRepo.js`; `isDecisionTarget` from `../services/extraction/runTypes.js`.

### Task 22: `superseded_by_human_edit` on the contacts PATCH path

**Files:** Modify `app/src/routes/contacts.ts:118-126`, `:818`, `:1318-1327`; `app/test/aiRunVerdicts.test.ts` (append)

**Interfaces:** Produces `ContactsRouterDeps.aiRunsRepo?: AiRunsRepo`.

**The rule, from spec 7.3 as amended - and note exactly where the value comparison applies.** A human PATCH edit yields `superseded_by_human_edit` for eleven of the twelve targets, regardless of value. **For `type` ONLY, the verdict depends on the VALUE**: `accepted` when the patched type equals the suggestion's `suggestedValue`, `superseded_by_human_edit` when it differs. A human triaging a contact to `landlord` after the model suggested `tenant` has REJECTED that suggestion, and recording it as an acceptance would invert the accuracy signal this whole feature exists to produce.

**Do NOT generalize the comparison to the other eleven targets.** The spec considered and rejected a general equality rule: it cannot even fire for `voucherSize` (stored as a number), `porting` (boolean) or `address` (an object), whose stored forms are not string-comparable to `suggestedValue`. For those eleven, a PATCH is a human edit that supersedes regardless of value.

**Without instrumenting this surface, every decision a human resolves by editing the field directly sits `pending` FOREVER** (spec 7.3).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

  it('stamps superseded_by_human_edit for every CHANGED field with a pending suggestion', async () => {
    const { app, setVerdict } = await makeWorld();
    await seedSuggestion({ contactId: 'c1', target: 'pets', suggestedValue: 'two cats', runId: 'run-1' });
    await seedSuggestion({ contactId: 'c1', target: 'tenure', suggestedValue: '3 years', runId: 'run-1' });
    await request(app).patch('/api/contacts/c1').send({ pets: 'a dog' }).expect(200);
    expect(setVerdict).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      'run-1', 'pets', 'superseded_by_human_edit', expect.objectContaining({ by: 'usr_test' }),
    );
    // tenure was not changed, so its decision is untouched.

    await seedSuggestion({ contactId: 'c1', target: 'type', suggestedValue: 'tenant', runId: 'run-1' });

    expect(setVerdict).toHaveBeenCalledWith('run-1', 'type', 'accepted', expect.anything());

  it('a `type` PATCH to a DIFFERENT value is a REJECTION - superseded_by_human_edit', async () => {
    // The case that matters most: a human triaging to landlord after the model

    // would invert the accuracy signal this feature exists to produce (7.3).
    const { app, setVerdict } = await makeWorld();

    await request(app).patch('/api/contacts/c1').send({ type: 'landlord' }).expect(200);

    expect(extraction.deleteSuggestion).toHaveBeenCalledWith('c1', 'pets');
  });

    const { app } = await makeWorld({ setVerdictImpl: async () => { throw new Error('ai_runs down'); } });
    await seedSuggestion({ contactId: 'c1', target: 'pets', suggestedValue: 'two cats', runId: 'run-1' });
    await request(app).patch('/api/contacts/c1').send({ pets: 'a dog' }).expect(200);

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Replace `app/src/routes/contacts.ts:1318-1327`. **Three SEPARATE try blocks**, so the new read can never suppress the existing delete:

```ts
    // conversation-fact-extraction (T8): a human edit supersedes any pending AI
    // suggestion for the same field (and a `type` change supersedes the type
    // recommendation). Best-effort - a suggestion-store hiccup never fails the PATCH.
    //
    // AI run log (design 7.3): READ the suggestion first, so its runId can carry
    // the verdict back to the run that produced it. Without this, every decision
    // a human resolves by editing the field directly sits `pending` forever, and
    // this is the ONLY route by which a `type` decision can reach `accepted`.
    //
    // THREE separate try blocks on purpose: the READ is new, and putting it in
    // the delete's try would let a read failure skip a delete that happens today.
    const verdictAt = new Date().toISOString();
    for (const f of parsed.changedFields) {
      let pending: SuggestionItem | undefined;
      try {
        pending = await extraction.getSuggestion(contactId, f);
      } catch (err) {
        log.warn({ err, contactId, field: f }, 'extraction getSuggestion (human edit) failed (best-effort)');
      }
      try {
        await extraction.deleteSuggestion(contactId, f);
      } catch (err) {
        log.warn({ err, contactId, field: f }, 'extraction deleteSuggestion (human edit) failed (best-effort)');
      }
      if (pending?.runId === undefined || !isDecisionTarget(f)) continue;
      // Spec 7.3 (amended): a PATCH supersedes - EXCEPT for `type`, where the
      // verdict depends on the VALUE. The accept route refuses `type`, so this
      // is its only path to `accepted`; but a human triaging to `landlord` after
      // the model suggested `tenant` has REJECTED it, and recording that as an
      // acceptance would invert the accuracy signal.
      //
      // CONFINED TO `type`. A general equality rule was considered and rejected:
      // it cannot fire for voucherSize (number), porting (boolean) or address
      // (object), whose stored forms are not string-comparable to
      // suggestedValue - so for the other eleven a PATCH supersedes regardless.
      const patchedType = parsed.patch[f];
      const verdict =
        f === 'type'
          ? typeof patchedType === 'string' && patchedType === pending.suggestedValue
            ? 'accepted'
            : 'superseded_by_human_edit'
          : 'superseded_by_human_edit';
      try {
        await aiRuns.setVerdict(pending.runId, f, verdict, {
          at: verdictAt,
          ...(req.user?.userId !== undefined && { by: req.user.userId }),
        });
      } catch (err) {
        log.warn({ err, contactId, field: f }, 'ai run verdict stamp failed (best-effort)');
      }
    }
```

Add `aiRunsRepo?: AiRunsRepo;` to `ContactsRouterDeps` near `:122`, `const aiRuns = deps.aiRunsRepo ?? createAiRunsRepo({ logger: deps.logger });` near `:818`, and the imports (`createAiRunsRepo`, `type AiRunsRepo`, `type SuggestionItem`, `isDecisionTarget`).

**Watch item - the auto-advance interaction.** `app/src/routes/contacts.ts:1242-1246` and `:1263-1279` PUSH `'status'` onto `parsed.changedFields` when a re-type auto-advances it, so a re-type also resolves any pending `status` decision as `superseded_by_human_edit`. That is correct - the human's action did supersede it. Do not special-case it.

### Task 23: `superseded` stamped by the JOB from `ApplyOutcome.displaced`

**Files:** Modify `app/src/jobs/extraction.ts` (add `stampSuperseded`; call it in `runDueExtractions` after `recordRun` - the line Task 20 Step 2 deferred); `app/test/extractionJob.test.ts` (append); `dashboard/src/api/types.ts`

**Why the job and not `apply.ts`** (spec 7.3): `apply.ts` is `putSuggestion`'s caller, so "the caller does it" would hand apply an `ai_runs` dependency the write-path section denies. - [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```ts
/**
 * Stamp `superseded` on every EARLIER run whose pending suggestion this run
 * displaced. Written HERE, not inside putSuggestion and not by apply.ts:
 * apply.ts is putSuggestion's caller, so "the caller does it" would hand apply
 * an ai_runs dependency the write-path section denies - and a stamp failure must
 * never be mistaken for a suggestion failure, because putSuggestionSafe treats a
 * throw as a failed suggestion and that suppresses the load-bearing
 * `suggestion.updated` emit (design 7.3).
 *
 * Best-effort PER STAMP: one bad stamp never blocks the rest.
 */
async function stampSuperseded(deps: ExtractionJobDeps, draft: RunDraft): Promise<void> {
  if (draft.displaced.length === 0) return;
  const at = deps.now();
  for (const { target, runId } of draft.displaced) {
    if (!isDecisionTarget(target)) continue;
    try {
      await deps.aiRuns.setVerdict(runId, target, 'superseded', { at });
    } catch (err) {
      deps.logger.warn(
        { conversationId: draft.conversationId, runId, target, err },
        'ai run superseded stamp failed (best-effort)',
      );
    }
  }
}
```

and add `await stampSuperseded(deps, draft);` immediately after `await recordRun(deps, outcome, draft);` in the `runDueExtractions` loop.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Add to the `MessagesRepo` interface immediately after `listByConversation` (`:671`):

```ts
  /**
   * Point-get ONE message by its exact key. Added for the AI run log's window
   * rehydration (design 2026-08-06 section 9): the run stores message IDS, not
   * text, so the detail view reads them back on demand. Returns undefined for a
   * deleted message - the view renders that as unavailable, and the stored hash
   * and char count still prove what was sent.
   */
  getByTsMsgId(conversationId: string, tsMsgId: string): Promise<MessageItem | undefined>;
  /**
   * BATCH point-get for a whole window (up to MAX_TRANSCRIPT_MESSAGES ids),
   * keyed by tsMsgId. Chunked at the BatchGetItem 100-key limit, with an
   * UnprocessedKeys retry. Missing ids are simply absent from the map.
   */
  getManyByTsMsgIds(conversationId: string, tsMsgIds: string[]): Promise<Map<string, MessageItem>>;
```

Add the implementations immediately after `listByConversation` (`:1511`):

```ts
    async getByTsMsgId(conversationId, tsMsgId) {
      const { Item } = await doc.send(
        new GetCommand({ TableName: table, Key: { conversationId, tsMsgId } }),
      );
      return Item as MessageItem | undefined;
    },

    async getManyByTsMsgIds(conversationId, tsMsgIds) {
      const out = new Map<string, MessageItem>();
      if (tsMsgIds.length === 0) return out;
      // BatchGetItem caps at 100 keys per request.
      for (let i = 0; i < tsMsgIds.length; i += 100) {
        let keys = tsMsgIds.slice(i, i + 100).map((tsMsgId) => ({ conversationId, tsMsgId }));
        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 4 && keys.length > 0; attempt += 1) {
          if (attempt > 0) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 25 * 2 ** (attempt - 1)));
          const res = await doc.send(new BatchGetCommand({ RequestItems: { [table]: { Keys: keys } } }));
          for (const item of (res.Responses?.[table] ?? []) as MessageItem[]) out.set(item.tsMsgId, item);
          keys = (res.UnprocessedKeys?.[table]?.Keys ?? []) as Array<{ conversationId: string; tsMsgId: string }>;
        }
      }
      return out;
    },
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the list endpoint**

Create `app/src/routes/aiRuns.ts`:

```ts
// AI run log API (design 2026-08-06 section 9). ADMIN ONLY ON THE SERVER -
// client-side route guarding alone is not authorization.
//
//   GET /api/ai-runs?scope=&before=&from=&to=&limit=  -> 200 { runs, nextBefore? }
//   GET /api/ai-runs/:runId                           -> 200 { run, window }
//
// SCOPE IS A SINGLE EXCLUSIVE SELECTOR, not a filter matrix: one Query has one
// hash key, so global / a contact / a conversation / an outcome cannot be
// combined. Only the date range composes (a BETWEEN on sortKey). Presenting
// these as independent checkboxes would promise a query the index cannot serve.
//
// PII: run records carry contact PII by design (rawText, rawResult, decision
// values). The LIST projects a summary WITHOUT them; the DETAIL returns them to
// an authenticated admin. Nothing here is ever logged.
import { Router } from 'express';
import { mergeContext } from '../lib/context.js';
import { logger as defaultLogger, type Logger } from '../lib/logger.js';
import { requireRole } from '../middleware/auth.js';
import {
  createAiRunsRepo,
  DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE,
  type AiRunRecord,
  type AiRunsRepo,
} from '../repos/aiRunsRepo.js';
import { createMessagesRepo, type MessagesRepo } from '../repos/messagesRepo.js';
import { DECISION_TARGETS } from '../services/extraction/runTypes.js';

export interface AiRunsRouterDeps {
  logger?: Logger;
  aiRunsRepo?: AiRunsRepo;
  messagesRepo?: Pick<MessagesRepo, 'getManyByTsMsgIds'>;
}

/** Hard ceiling on a page, so a hand-crafted limit cannot fan out a BatchGet. */
const MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 100;
/** The exact scope forms the byEntity index can serve (design 5.3). */
const SCOPE_PATTERN =
  /^(global|outcome#(applied|no_op|skipped|failed)|conversations#[\w.-]+|contacts#[\w.-]+)$/;

/** Per-outcome counts for a list row - never the values themselves. */
function decisionCounts(run: AiRunRecord): Record<string, number> {
  const counts = { wrote: 0, suggested: 0, dropped: 0, no_finding: 0, not_addressed: 0, pending: 0 };
  for (const target of DECISION_TARGETS) {
    const d = run.decisions[target];
    if (d === undefined) continue;
    counts[d.outcome] += 1;
    if (d.verdict === 'pending') counts.pending += 1;
  }
  return counts;
}

export function createAiRunsRouter(deps: AiRunsRouterDeps = {}): Router {
  const log = deps.logger ?? defaultLogger;
  const aiRuns = deps.aiRunsRepo ?? createAiRunsRepo({ logger: deps.logger });
  const messages = deps.messagesRepo ?? createMessagesRepo({ logger: deps.logger });

  const router = Router();
  // Every route here is admin-only (a VA gets 403). Mirrors routes/system.ts:42.
  router.use(requireRole('admin'));

  router.get('/', async (req, res) => {
    const scope = String(req.query['scope'] ?? 'global');
    if (!SCOPE_PATTERN.test(scope)) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: 'invalid_scope' });
      return;
    }
    const rawLimit = Number(req.query['limit']);
    const limit =
      Number.isFinite(rawLimit) && rawLimit > 0
        ? Math.min(Math.floor(rawLimit), MAX_PAGE_SIZE)
        : DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE;
    const before = typeof req.query['before'] === 'string' ? req.query['before'] : undefined;
    const from = typeof req.query['from'] === 'string' ? req.query['from'] : undefined;
    const to = typeof req.query['to'] === 'string' ? req.query['to'] : undefined;

    const page = await aiRuns.listByEntity(scope, {
      limit,
      ...(before !== undefined && { before }),
      ...(from !== undefined && { from }),
      ...(to !== undefined && { to }),
    });
    const runs = page.entries.map((e) =>
      e.expired
        ? { runId: e.runId, sortKey: e.sortKey, expired: true as const }
        : {
            runId: e.runId,
            sortKey: e.sortKey,
            expired: false as const,
            startedAt: e.run.startedAt,
            durationMs: e.run.durationMs,
            conversationId: e.run.conversationId,
            ...(e.run.contactId !== undefined && { contactId: e.run.contactId }),
            trigger: e.run.trigger,
            outcome: e.run.outcome,
            ...(e.run.skipReason !== undefined && { skipReason: e.run.skipReason }),
            ...(e.run.error !== undefined && { errorKind: e.run.error.kind }),
            driver: e.run.driver,
            ...(e.run.model !== undefined && { model: e.run.model }),
            decisionCounts: decisionCounts(e.run),
            notedLines: e.run.notedLines,
          },
    );
    log.debug({ scope, count: runs.length }, 'ai run log listed');
    res.json({ runs, ...(page.nextBefore !== undefined && { nextBefore: page.nextBefore }) });
  });

  return router;
}
```

### Task 27: `GET /api/ai-runs/:runId` - detail with BATCHED window rehydration

**Files:** Modify `app/src/routes/aiRuns.ts`, `app/test/aiRunsApi.test.ts`

**Interfaces:** Produces this response body, and **it is a two-key envelope, NOT a bare run record** - the rehydrated window rides ALONGSIDE `run`, not inside it, because `run.window.messages` stays exactly what was stored:

```ts
// The wire shape. Task 28 mirrors this VERBATIM as AiRunDetailResponse.
{
  run: AiRunRecord,                                   // exactly as stored
  window: { messages: (RunWindowMessage & { available: boolean; text?: string })[] }
}
```

plus `404 { error: 'run_not_found' }` for an unknown or TTLd run. The top-level key set is asserted explicitly below, because no other layer catches a mismatch: the hooks are mocked in Task 28, Task 29 is a specification step, and the e2e reaches the text only through the rendered DOM.

**Two things the naive version gets wrong.** A window holds up to 50 ids, so a per-message point-get would fire 50 round trips per page view: use `getManyByTsMsgIds` (Task 24). And a CALL message carries its text in `transcript`, not `body` - reading `body` only would render every call in the window blank, which is exactly the message kind the window's `noContent` and hash machinery exists to reason about.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Append inside `createAiRunsRouter`, before `return router;`:

```ts
  router.get('/:runId', async (req, res) => {
    const runId = String(req.params['runId'] ?? '');
    mergeContext({ runId });
    const run = await aiRuns.getRun(runId);
    if (run === undefined) {
      // Unknown, or already TTLd at 90 days.
      res.status(404).json({ error: 'run_not_found' });
      return;
    }
    // Rehydrate the window's message text on demand - the record stores IDS, not
    // a second copy of the text (design 6.1). ONE batched read: a window holds up
    // to MAX_TRANSCRIPT_MESSAGES ids, and a per-message point-get would fire that
    // many round trips per page view. A deleted message renders as unavailable;
    // its stored hash and char count still prove what was sent.
    const stored = run.window?.messages ?? [];
    let byId = new Map<string, Awaited<ReturnType<typeof messages.getManyByTsMsgIds>> extends Map<string, infer M> ? M : never>();
    if (stored.length > 0) {
      try {
        byId = await messages.getManyByTsMsgIds(run.conversationId, stored.map((m) => m.tsMsgId));
      } catch (err) {
        // Degrade the WINDOW, never the page.
        log.warn({ runId, err }, 'ai run log: window rehydration failed');
      }
    }
    const messagesOut = stored.map((m) => {
      const row = byId.get(m.tsMsgId);
      // A CALL carries its text in `transcript`, not `body`.
      const text = row === undefined ? undefined : row.type === 'call' ? row.transcript : row.body;
      return { ...m, available: row !== undefined, ...(text !== undefined && { text }) };
    });
    res.json({ run, window: { messages: messagesOut } });
  });
```

Simplify the `byId` declaration to `let byId = new Map<string, MessageItem>();` with a type-only `import type { MessageItem } from '../repos/messagesRepo.js';` - the conditional type above is only there to show the shape; do not ship it.

### Task 28: Types, endpoints and the data hooks

**Files:** Modify `dashboard/src/api/types.ts` (append an `// --- AI run log (/api/ai-runs) ---` banner section, per the convention at `:150`), `dashboard/src/api/endpoints.ts` (append); create `dashboard/src/routes/settings/aiRuns/useAiRuns.ts`, `useAiRuns.test.ts`

**Interfaces:** Produces `AiRunOutcome`, `AiRunScope`, `AiRunListRow`, `AiRunListPage`, `AiRunDecision`, `AiRunWindowMessage`, `AiRunRecordView`, **`AiRunDetailResponse`**; `listAiRuns(params, signal)`, `getAiRun(runId, signal)`; `useAiRunList(params)`, `useAiRun(runId)`.

**The detail endpoint returns a TWO-KEY ENVELOPE, not a bare run record.** Task 27 pins `{ run, window: { messages } }` with an explicit key-set assertion; this mirror must match it exactly, or `useAiRun` reads `run.startedAt` off an object that has no such field and the page renders blanks with no type error anywhere. Declare it as two named types, never as `AiRunDetail = AiRunRecord`:

```ts
/** The stored run, EXACTLY as the server holds it (mirrors AiRunRecord). */
export interface AiRunRecordView { /* runId, startedAt, ..., window?, decisions, notedLines */ }
/** One window message as the DETAIL endpoint returns it: the stored fields plus
 *  the two rehydration fields. `text` is absent for a deleted message. */
export interface AiRunWindowMessage { /* tsMsgId, type, direction, tier, capChars?, truncated?, chars?, hash?, available: boolean, text?: string */ }
/** GET /api/ai-runs/:runId - the two-key envelope. The rehydrated window rides
 *  ALONGSIDE `run`; `run.window` stays exactly what was stored. */
export interface AiRunDetailResponse {
  run: AiRunRecordView;
  window: { messages: AiRunWindowMessage[] };
}
```

```ts
/** GET /api/ai-runs - one page of the admin AI run log (admin-only). */
export function listAiRuns(
  params: { scope?: AiRunScope; before?: string; from?: string; to?: string; limit?: number } = {},
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<AiRunListPage> {
  return request<AiRunListPage>('/api/ai-runs', {
    query: { scope: params.scope, before: params.before, from: params.from, to: params.to, limit: params.limit },
    ...(signal !== undefined && { signal }),
  });
}

/** GET /api/ai-runs/:runId - one run with its rehydrated window (admin-only). */
export function getAiRun(runId: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<AiRunDetailResponse> {
  return request<AiRunDetailResponse>(`/api/ai-runs/${encodeURIComponent(runId)}`, {
    ...(signal !== undefined && { signal }),
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the hooks (SPECIFICATION step)**

`useAiRuns.ts` exports two hooks. Copy the AbortController structure of `useSystemFlags` (`dashboard/src/routes/settings/useSystemStatus.ts:35-70`) VERBATIM, including the `// eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` this repo's lint config requires (`useSystemStatus.ts:58`).

- `useAiRunList(params: { scope: AiRunScope; from?: string; to?: string })` -> `{ rows: AiRunListRow[]; status: 'loading' | 'ready' | 'error'; hasMore: boolean; loadingMore: boolean; loadMore(): void; retry(): void }`. Resets `rows` on any `params` change; `loadMore` appends using the stored `nextBefore`.
- `useAiRun(runId: string | undefined)` -> `{ detail: AiRunDetailResponse | undefined; status: 'idle' | 'loading' | 'ready' | 'error'; retry(): void }`. Name the field `detail`, not `run`, so a consumer reaching for `detail.run.startedAt` cannot silently be handed the envelope. `runId === undefined` stays `'idle'` and never fetches.

- [ ] **Step 5: Build the three components (SPECIFICATION step)**

Copy `dashboard/src/routes/placements/PlacementDetail.tsx:389-463` as the structural pattern.

- **Shell.** Root `<div className={shell.page}>` with `import shell from '../../../ui/twoPaneShell.module.css'`. Header: `<h2 id="ai-runs-heading">AI run log</h2>` plus a config strip fed by `useSystemFlags()` (`dashboard/src/routes/settings/useSystemStatus.ts:35-70`) showing `aiExtractionEnabled`, `aiExtractionDriver`, `aiExtractionModel`, `aiExtractionPromptFingerprint` - spec 9's whole point, so an empty log is never read as "the AI found nothing".
- **Config-strip DOM contract - PINNED, because the e2e depends on it.** Each item renders with an accessible name of exactly `` `${label}: ${value}` ``, e.g. `aria-label="Extraction driver: fake"`, using these four labels verbatim: `AI extraction` (value `on`/`off`), `Extraction driver`, `Extraction model`, `Prompt fingerprint`. **The name must include the VALUE.** A strip that exposes only the static label lets `getByText(/extraction driver/i)` pass whether or not the flag is plumbed through - which is precisely the cannot-fail species this plan exists to avoid, and it slipped through one revision of this very step. The component test `renders the config strip so an empty log is never misread` asserts `getByLabel('Extraction driver: fake')`, not the label alone, and Task 30's e2e asserts the same string.
- **Panes.** `shell.segMobile` toggle between `'list'` and `'detail'` via `useState`; both panes stay MOUNTED (the CSS `display:none`s one below 860px), so gate any side-effecting pane with `useTwoPaneNarrow()` (`dashboard/src/ui/twoPaneShell.ts:34-47`). Body is `shell.body` > `shell.left` (list) + `shell.right` > `shell.rightInner` (detail).
- **URL state.** Scope and selection live in the query string via `useSearchParams` - the idiom at `dashboard/src/routes/placements/PlacementsPage.tsx:53-61`: `?scope=` and `?run=<runId>`. Keep the tab path exactly `/settings/ai-runs` so `SettingsPage.tsx:20`'s prefix match still highlights the tab.
- **Scope control.** A radio group or `<select>` - **never checkboxes.** One Query has one hash key (spec 9); independent checkboxes would promise a query the index cannot serve. Options: Global, Applied, No-op, Skipped, Failed, plus the current contact/conversation when arrived at from a deep link. A separate date-range pair composes with any scope.
- **List rows.** `<ul aria-label="AI runs">` of buttons showing time, trigger, outcome chip, driver, contact and the decision counts; an expired row renders a tombstone. `Load more` gated on `hasMore`/`loadingMore`, per `dashboard/src/routes/inbox/Inbox.tsx:77-86`.
- **Detail pane.** Run header (contact, time, trigger, driver/model/fingerprint, tokens, duration); the window table (tier, truncation, hash status, `excluded`-with-cause and `noContent` lists, a `windowCappedAtLimit` note) which **must label a `detail: 'light'` window as a skip window and omit the byte columns entirely** rather than rendering blanks; the decisions table (target / proposed / was / outcome / verdict / reason), rendering a `dropped` row with no `dropReason` as "unexplained"; collapsed `<details>` for raw text and parsed result.
- **States.** `'loading' | 'ready' | 'error'` with `<Spinner center />`, a `role="alert"` error block with Retry, and an empty state - per `dashboard/src/routes/placements/PlacementsPage.tsx:119-137`.
- **CSS.** A module beside the component, values from `dashboard/src/ui/tokens.css` only. **Both tables sit inside an `overflow-x: auto` wrapper** so the page body never scrolls horizontally.

# SLICE 16 - e2e, docs and the final gate

### Task 30: The end-to-end spec

**Files:** Create `e2e/tests/dashboard-next/ai-run-log.spec.ts`; modify `e2e/tests/dashboard-next/settings.spec.ts:36-38`

**Interfaces:** Consumes `extractionTick` (`e2e/fixtures/extraction.ts:27-31`) and `sendExtractSms` (`:50-62`); the `devLoginAs` idiom at `e2e/tests/dashboard-next/pool-numbers-admin.spec.ts:29-34`; the VA-bounce idiom at `:118-140`.

**All `EXTRACT:` payloads here are SCHEMA-SHAPED** (Task 9), because the run log reads `rawText` through `parseExtractionOps`. A payload that omits `op` would record every decision as `not_addressed` and the assertions below would pass while verifying nothing.

**WHAT IS AND IS NOT PRODUCIBLE END TO END - read this before writing a line.** Several obvious-looking fixtures cannot produce the state they name, and the resulting test passes while verifying nothing. Verified against the code:

| State | Producible? | Why |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `applied` / `no_op` run | **Yes** | inbound SMS + `extractionTick` |
| `failed` run | No (accepted gap) | the fake driver never fails and no seam injects one; Task 19's unit tests cover all five `error.kind`s |
| `skipped` / `no_new_client` + a light window WITH message rows | **No** | `claim()` REMOVEs `_duePartition` and `dueAt` (`app/src/repos/extractionRepo.ts:231`), so a second `extractionTick` finds ZERO due rows and no run happens at all. And a due row can only ever be armed by content that satisfies the freshness gate (`sms`/`email`, from an inbound) or on a channel that BYPASSES it (`voice`/`triage`) - so `no_new_client` is unreachable from any seam. Task 19's `records a no_new_client SKIP with a LIGHT window` test is the ONLY coverage. |
| `skipped` / `empty_window` + an EMPTY light window | **Yes** | a contact with a message-less conversation, then a triage PATCH to `tenant` (which arms a `triage`-channel row that bypasses the gate, `app/src/routes/contacts.ts:1431`) -> `fresh.length === 0`. This is the only e2e that touches `detail: 'light'` at all. |
| `skipped` / `ineligible_type` | Not attempted | an inbound SMS only arms a row for `tenant_1to1` / `unknown_1to1` (`app/src/routes/webhooks/twilio.ts:1158-1161`), so a landlord inbound arms nothing. Reachable by arming while `unknown` then re-typing, but it carries NO window and adds nothing the unit tests miss. |
| `excluded` cause `char_budget` | **Yes** | three inbound SMS of `NEW_MESSAGE_CHAR_CAP` (30_000) chars: two fill `WINDOW_CHAR_BUDGET` (60_000) exactly and the third overflows, dropping the OLDEST |
| `noContent` | **Yes** | plant a call with an EMPTY transcript. Chain verified end to end: `planTranscribedCall` with `sentences: [{ text: '', mediaChannel: 1 }]` passes the dev validator, which requires only a non-empty ARRAY of `{ text: string, mediaChannel: number }` and `''` is a string (`app/src/routes/dev.ts:427-441`); `joinViSentences` sees one distinct channel and returns `turns.map(turnText).join('\n')` = `''` (`app/src/services/voiceTranscripts.ts:89-92`); `setCallTranscript`'s guard is `attribute_not_exists(transcript) OR transcript = :empty`, so a fresh call row accepts it and stamps `transcript_status = 'completed'` with `transcript = ''` (`app/src/repos/messagesRepo.ts:1255-1260`); and `toUtterances` returns `[]` on `transcript_status !== 'completed' OR !m.transcript` - the SECOND disjunct fires, because `''` is falsy (`app/src/jobs/extraction.ts:131`). No shared repo method is widened. The plant also arms a gate-bypassing `voice` row, so the run proceeds to the model. |
| `excluded` cause `age_30d` | Not asserted - and the reason is NOT "unproducible" | It is trivially producible, but only as a FIXTURE ARTIFACT: the lean seed's three tenant-conversation messages carry `ts` and no `created_at` (`app/src/lib/seed/lean.ts:192-220`), and `undefined >= cutoff` is `false`, so they are ALWAYS excluded as `age_30d`. Production never produces that - `messagesRepo.append` stamps `created_at: now` on every row (`app/src/repos/messagesRepo.ts:888`). An e2e asserting `age_30d` against the seeded conversation would be pinning a seed-data bug, not the feature. The tests below build FRESH conversations, whose messages all carry `created_at`, so `age_30d` legitimately never appears in them. Covered by Task 16's `separates the two exclusion causes` unit test. |

Spec 11 asks for `excluded` causes AND `noContent` in the e2e. `char_budget` and `noContent` are asserted; `age_30d` is deliberately not, for the fixture-artifact reason above. That single deviation is recorded in SPEC CONCERNS #3 and filed as a `docs/issues/` entry in Task 31 - it is NOT silently dropped.

**Isolation:** every test below reseeds first and then scopes the log query by `?scope=contacts%23<id>`, so `.first()` can never pick up a row another test in the same file produced. A `.first()` over an unreseeded, unscoped list is exactly how an e2e stops being able to fail.

**NEVER touch the lean seed.** `app/src/lib/seed/lean.ts` is BYTE-STABLE BY DESIGN and shared by every other e2e spec; editing it to make a fixture convenient would break other suites. Build every fixture IN-TEST, which is the convention `e2e/tests/flows/conversation-fact-extraction.spec.ts:13-14` already states: "Each test builds a FRESH contact (unique phone) so the lean seed stays byte-stable (we never mutate a seeded contact)."

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the spec**

```ts
// AI run log e2e (design section 11). Drives a message through the FAKE driver's
// EXTRACT: marker protocol - which emits a synthetic rawText - so this exercises
// the REAL decisions mechanism rather than only its absent-rawText fallback.
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { extractionTick, planTranscribedCall, sendExtractSms } from '../../fixtures/extraction.js';
import { reseed } from '../../fixtures/reseed.js';

const NEXT = process.env['E2E_DASHBOARD_URL'] ?? 'http://127.0.0.1:5174';

async function devLoginAs(page: Page, email: string): Promise<void> {
  const res = await page.request.post(`${NEXT}/auth/dev-login`, { data: { email } });
  expect(res.ok()).toBeTruthy();
  await page.goto(`${NEXT}/`);
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Today' })).toBeVisible();
}

// A write that lands, plus an EXPLICIT decline on another target, so the run
// records both a `wrote` and a real `no_finding` - which only happens if
// parseExtractionOps actually read the synthetic rawText.
const MARKER = {
  fields: {
    pets: { op: 'write', value: 'two cats', reason: 'said so' },
    tenure: { op: 'none', value: '', reason: '' },
  },
};

/** Open the log scoped to ONE contact, so `.first()` is unambiguous. */
async function openRunFor(page: Page, contactId: string, outcome: RegExp): Promise<void> {
  await page.goto(`${NEXT}/settings/ai-runs?scope=${encodeURIComponent(`contacts#${contactId}`)}`);
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'AI run log' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: outcome }).first().click();
}

test.beforeEach(async ({ request }) => {
  await reseed(request); // isolation: no row from a sibling test survives
});

test('an extraction run appears with its window, decisions and rehydrated text', async ({ page, request }) => {
  await devLoginAs(page, 'founder@example.com');
  // FRESH tenant, never a seeded one - the lean seed must stay byte-stable.
  const { contactId, phone } = await createTenant(request, { firstName: 'Runlog' });
  await sendExtractSms(request, phone, MARKER);
  expect((await extractionTick(request)).processed).toBeGreaterThan(0);

  await openRunFor(page, contactId, /applied/i);
  // The header states the config, so an empty log is never misread. Assert the
  // VALUE, not the label: `getByText(/extraction driver/i)` would match the
  // STATIC LABEL and pass whether or not aiExtractionDriver is plumbed through.
  // The accessible name is pinned as `<Label>: <value>` in Task 29 Step 5.
  await expect(page.getByLabel('Extraction driver: fake')).toBeVisible();

  // WINDOW: the message that reached the model, with its tier - and its
  // REHYDRATED TEXT, which is the only end-to-end proof that the detail
  // endpoint's two-key envelope survives the wire into the page (Task 27).
  const windowTable = page.getByRole('table', { name: /window/i });
  await expect(windowTable).toBeVisible();
  await expect(windowTable).toContainText('new');
  await expect(windowTable).toContainText('EXTRACT:'); // the stored body, read back

  // DECISIONS: the write, the EXPLICIT decline, and a target never mentioned.
  // `no finding` appears ONLY if parseExtractionOps actually read the fake
  // driver's synthetic rawText - it is the assertion that proves the mechanism.
  await expect(page.getByRole('row', { name: /pets/ })).toContainText('wrote');
  await expect(page.getByRole('row', { name: /tenure/ })).toContainText(/no finding/i);
  await expect(page.getByRole('row', { name: /housingAuthority/ })).toContainText(/not addressed/i);
});

test('the window records a char_budget exclusion with its CAUSE', async ({ page, request }) => {
  // Two 30k-char messages fill WINDOW_CHAR_BUDGET (60_000) exactly; the third
  // overflows and the OLDEST is dropped.
  await devLoginAs(page, 'founder@example.com');
  const { contactId, phone } = await createTenant(request, { firstName: 'Budget' });
  const filler = 'x'.repeat(30_000);
  for (let i = 0; i < 2; i += 1) await sendPlainSms(request, phone, `${filler}${i}`);
  await sendExtractSms(request, phone, MARKER);
  await extractionTick(request);

  await openRunFor(page, contactId, /applied/i);
  const excluded = page.getByRole('region', { name: /excluded/i });
  await expect(excluded).toBeVisible();
  await expect(excluded).toContainText('char_budget');
});

test('a call with an EMPTY transcript is recorded as noContent, never as read', async ({ page, request }) => {
  // A call that carried NOTHING to the model must appear under no-content and
  // NEVER as a read message - recording it as read would be a false claim that
  // the model saw a call it never saw (design 6.1).
  //
  // The chain, verified: an empty `text` passes the dev validator (it checks
  // `typeof text === 'string'`, dev.ts:427-441); joinViSentences returns ''
  // for a single-channel turn (voiceTranscripts.ts:89-92); setCallTranscript's
  // `attribute_not_exists(transcript) OR transcript = :empty` guard accepts it
  // and stamps transcript_status='completed' (messagesRepo.ts:1255-1260); and
  // toUtterances returns [] because `!m.transcript` is true for ''
  // (extraction.ts:131). The plant also arms a gate-bypassing `voice` row.
  await devLoginAs(page, 'founder@example.com');
  const { contactId, phone } = await createTenant(request, { firstName: 'Silent' });
  await sendExtractSms(request, phone, MARKER); // gives the run something to decide
  const conversationId = await conversationIdFor(request, contactId);
  await planTranscribedCall(request, {
    conversationId,
    callSid: `CAempty${Date.now()}`,
    sentences: [{ text: '', mediaChannel: 1 }],
  });
  await extractionTick(request);

  await openRunFor(page, contactId, /applied|no.?op/i);
  const noContent = page.getByRole('region', { name: /no content/i });
  await expect(noContent).toBeVisible();
  // PRECONDITION + PAYLOAD in one: the call id appears under no-content, and
  // the SAME id must NOT appear in the read-messages table.
  const callRow = await noContent.textContent();
  expect(callRow).toMatch(/#c|call/i);
  await expect(page.getByRole('table', { name: /window/i })).not.toContainText('call');
});

test('a SKIP records a LIGHT window and no decisions', async ({ page, request }) => {
  // The ONLY e2e that reaches detail: 'light'. A `no_new_client` skip is NOT
  // producible - claim() removes the due keys so a second tick finds nothing,
  // and no seam arms an sms-channel row without new client content. So this
  // uses `empty_window`: a contact whose conversation has ZERO messages,
  // triaged to tenant, which arms a triage-channel row that BYPASSES the gate.
  await devLoginAs(page, 'founder@example.com');
  // A FRESH contact created as `unknown`, given a conversation but no messages,
  // then triaged to tenant. createTenant() defaults to type tenant, so pass the
  // type through or use the same POST /api/contacts shape with type 'unknown'.
  const { contactId } = await createUnknown(request, { firstName: 'Empty' });
  await page.request.post(`${NEXT}/api/contacts/${contactId}/conversation`); // no messages
  await page.request.patch(`${NEXT}/api/contacts/${contactId}`, { data: { type: 'tenant' } });
  await extractionTick(request);

  // If the triage schedule did not arm, NO skipped row exists and this fails
  // RED - the safe direction. Never weaken it to a conditional assertion.
  await openRunFor(page, contactId, /skipped/i);
  await expect(page.getByText(/empty window/i)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText(/skip window/i)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('table', { name: /decisions/i })).toHaveCount(0);
});

test('accepting a suggestion stamps the verdict on the run', async ({ page, request }) => {
  // `op: 'suggest'` routes to the suggest path REGARDLESS of whether the field
  // is occupied (apply.ts:189-211 - the equal-to-current guard only fires when
  // a current value EXISTS and matches), so a fresh tenant with no `pets` is a
  // valid fixture. The `pending` assertion is still the precondition check.
  await devLoginAs(page, 'founder@example.com');
  const { contactId, phone } = await createTenant(request, { firstName: 'Accept' });
  await sendExtractSms(request, phone, {
    fields: { pets: { op: 'suggest', value: 'three dogs', reason: 'conflicts' } },
  });
  await extractionTick(request);
  await openRunFor(page, contactId, /applied/i);
  await expect(page.getByRole('row', { name: /pets/ })).toContainText(/pending/i);

  await page.request.post(`${NEXT}/api/contacts/${contactId}/suggestions/pets/accept`);
  await openRunFor(page, contactId, /applied/i);
  await expect(page.getByRole('row', { name: /pets/ })).toContainText(/accepted/i);
});

test('editing a field via PATCH stamps superseded_by_human_edit', async ({ page, request }) => {
  await devLoginAs(page, 'founder@example.com');
  const { contactId, phone } = await createTenant(request, { firstName: 'Superseded' });
  await sendExtractSms(request, phone, {
    fields: { pets: { op: 'suggest', value: 'three dogs', reason: 'conflicts' } },
  });
  await extractionTick(request);
  await page.request.patch(`${NEXT}/api/contacts/${contactId}`, { data: { pets: 'one hamster' } });

  await openRunFor(page, contactId, /applied/i);
  await expect(page.getByRole('row', { name: /pets/ })).toContainText(/superseded by human edit/i);
});

test('a `type` triage to a DIFFERENT value records a rejection, not an acceptance', async ({ page, request }) => {
  // The v7 amendment, end to end: the only target whose PATCH verdict depends
  // on the value. The model suggests `tenant`, the human triages to `landlord` -
  // that is a REJECTION. typeSuggestion only applies to an UNKNOWN contact
  // (apply.ts:348-361), and an inbound from an unrecognised number
  // auto-captures exactly that, so let the inbound create the contact.
  await devLoginAs(page, 'founder@example.com');
  const phone = uniquePhone();
  await sendExtractSms(request, phone, {
    typeSuggestion: { value: 'tenant', reason: 'asked about vouchers' },
  });
  const contactId = await findUnknownContactId(request, phone);
  await extractionTick(request);
  await page.request.patch(`${NEXT}/api/contacts/${contactId}`, { data: { type: 'landlord' } });

  await openRunFor(page, contactId, /applied/i);
  await expect(page.getByRole('row', { name: /type/ })).toContainText(/superseded by human edit/i);
});

test('a VA is bounced off /settings/ai-runs', async ({ page }) => {
  await devLoginAs(page, 'va@example.com');
  await page.goto(`${NEXT}/settings/ai-runs`);
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/settings\/templates$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'AI run log' })).toHaveCount(0);
});
```

**Fixtures are built IN-TEST, copied from the existing spec - never read out of the lean seed.** `e2e/tests/flows/conversation-fact-extraction.spec.ts` already carries every helper this spec needs; copy them (or export them into a shared fixture module):

| Helper | Source | Note |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `reseed(request)` | `e2e/fixtures/reseed.ts:3` | takes ONLY `request` - it has no profile argument |
| `uniquePhone()` | `conversation-fact-extraction.spec.ts:30-34` | `+1555` + a 5-digit stamp + a 2-digit sequence |
| `createTenant(request, { firstName, voucherSize?, status? })` | `:38-56` | returns `{ contactId, phone }`; POSTs `/api/contacts` with `type: 'tenant'` |
| `createUnknown(request, { firstName })` | NEW - clone `createTenant` with `type: 'unknown'` | needed only by the skip test |
| `findUnknownContactId(request, phone)` | `:58-75` | polls `/api/contacts?type=unknown` for an auto-captured contact |
| `sendPlainSms(request, phone, body)` | NEW - thin wrapper over `postInboundSms` in `e2e/fixtures/fakeTwilio.ts` | the same primitive `sendExtractSms` wraps at `e2e/fixtures/extraction.ts:50-62` |
| `conversationIdFor(request, contactId)` | NEW - `POST /api/contacts/:id/conversation` | the route `e2e/fixtures/extraction.ts:72` already names for resolving a 1:1 thread |

Use accessibility-first selectors throughout (`e2e/support/selectors.md`). Add `'AI run log'` to the tab-label loop at `e2e/tests/dashboard-next/settings.spec.ts:36-38`.

**The one precondition worth checking, and one that was WRONG.** `app/src/routes/webhooks/twilio.ts:1158-1161` arms a due row only for a `tenant_1to1` or `unknown_1to1` conversation, so every fixture above must be a tenant or an unknown - a landlord inbound silently produces zero runs and every assertion becomes unreachable. Do NOT re-add the previously-stated precondition that the contact must already carry a `pets` value: it was false (the seeded tenant has no `pets` - `app/src/lib/seed/lean.ts` sets `pets` only on UNITS, `:151` and `:174`) and unnecessary (`app/src/services/extraction/apply.ts:189-211` routes `op: 'suggest'` to the suggest path whether or not the field is occupied; the equal-to-current guard at `:197` fires only when a current value EXISTS and matches).

**Note the dev seam:** `app/src/routes/dev.ts:487` (`POST /__dev/voice/transcript-fixture`) arms a `voice`-channel row, so any spec that plants a transcript manufactures a `voice`-trigger run. Scope-filtered assertions must not assume every row is `sms`.

### Task 31: Docs, owed ops, memory and the `main` sync

**Files:** Modify `RUNBOOK.md`, `README.md` (the deviations table - `app/src/lib/tables.ts:10-19` requires logging a new table there), the feature's memory topic file, and its one-line `MEMORY.md` index entry

Skip `documentation/GLOSSARY.md`: `run`, `decision` and `verdict` are feature vocabulary, not domain nouns, and no new domain noun was introduced.

- [ ] **Step 1: RUNBOOK entry**

Cover: where the log lives (`/settings/ai-runs`, admin only); the 90-day TTL; that a pointer can outlive its run row and renders as expired; that the recorder is best-effort, so a MISSING record does not mean a missing run (it logs `ai run log write failed`); that timestamps are real wall clock even under the dev tick's simulated future poll clock; and that a `dropped` decision with no reason means an unexplained gap (grep the logs for `unexplained dropped decision`).

**RUNBOOK is operational only** - any bug, gap or deferral you found goes in `docs/issues/<slug>.md` (copy `docs/issues/_TEMPLATE.md`), never here. Run `npm run issues` afterwards to regenerate the gitignored index; never hand-maintain a list.

**File these three issues** (recorded deviations and a genuine seed defect, not open questions):

- `docs/issues/seed-messages-missing-created-at.md` - **a real fixture bug, found while planning this feature.** The lean seed's three tenant-conversation messages carry `ts` but no `created_at` (`app/src/lib/seed/lean.ts:192-220`), while production stamps `created_at` on every append (`app/src/repos/messagesRepo.ts:888`). Any code comparing `created_at` therefore sees `undefined` for seeded messages - the extraction window's 30-day cutoff (`undefined >= cutoff` is `false`) silently excludes ALL of them as `age_30d`. Not caused by this feature and NOT fixed here: the lean seed is byte-stable by design and shared by every e2e, so changing it is its own change with its own regression sweep. Severity low, but it is why this feature's e2e cannot honestly assert `age_30d`.
- `docs/issues/ai-run-log-no-e2e-age-30d-exclusion.md` - spec 11 asks the e2e to assert `excluded` causes; `char_budget` and `noContent` ARE asserted (Task 30), `age_30d` is not. Producing it would mean running against the seeded conversation and pinning the seed defect above rather than the feature. Unit coverage is Task 16's `separates the two exclusion causes`.
- `docs/issues/ai-run-log-no-e2e-light-window-rows.md` - a LIGHT window carrying message rows (`no_new_client`) has no end-to-end coverage: `claim()` REMOVEs `_duePartition` and `dueAt` (`app/src/repos/extractionRepo.ts:231`) so a second tick finds zero due rows, and every scheduler either supplies gate-satisfying content (`sms`/`email`) or runs on a channel that bypasses the gate (`voice`/`triage`). The `empty_window` e2e covers `detail: 'light'` with an EMPTY message list; Task 19's unit test is the only coverage of the populated case.

**2. Placeholder scan.** No "TBD", no "add error handling", no "similar to Task N", no "write tests for the above". The three SPECIFICATION steps are declared up front, listed by exact task and step number, and none is outside Slice 15. Where a step says "match the file's existing idiom" it names the exact `file:line` to copy from.

**3. Type consistency.** `RunDecision` / `DecisionTarget` / `DropReason` / `Verdict` / `RunWindow*` are defined ONCE in `runTypes.ts` (Task 2) and referenced by identical names in Tasks 3, 14, 16, 17, 19, 21-23, 26. `ApplyDecision` is defined once in `apply.ts` (Task 14) and consumed type-only by `decisions.ts` (Task 17). `ExtractionCall` / `ExtractionMeta` once (Task 9), consumed by 10, 11, 19. `PutSuggestionResult` once (Task 12), consumed by 12, 13. `RunDraft` / `newRunDraft` / `ProcessRowResult` once (Task 19), consumed by 20 and 23. `AiRunRecordInput` once (Task 3), consumed by 18, 20.

**4. Ordering seams.** `RunDraft` (with `error.attempts?`/`parked?`) and `newRunDraft` are declared in Task 19 Step 3, BEFORE Task 19 Step 4's `processRow` and Task 20's `recordRun` read them. `deps.now()` lands in Task 18, before any consumer. `applyEvents` is hoisted in Task 18 Step 5, before Task 20's backstop tests use it. `stampSuperseded` is deferred out of Task 20's loop body with an explicit note and added in Task 23. The one forward reference in a test - `parseExtractionOps` in Task 9 - is an explicit `it.skip` with a named unskip step (Task 15 Step 5) and a grep gate.

**5. Cannot-fail audit** (the sweep the top-of-plan section demands, run over every test in the plan). Fixed across the last two rounds: the two backstop tests throw from an UNWRAPPED site and assert `repo.complete` was never called, so they cannot silently exercise the ordinary `failed()` path; the PII guard asserts its log capture is non-empty and contains a known line before its two `not.toContain`s; the e2e reseeds per test and scopes by contact, so `.first()` cannot pick up a sibling test's row; the accept e2e asserts `pending` as a precondition, so a `suggest` op that silently became a write fails RED at the precondition; and **the config-strip assertion, which was itself a cannot-fail replacement, now asserts `getByLabel('Extraction driver: fake')` against a `` `${label}: ${value}` `` accessible name pinned in Task 29** rather than matching a static label. Verified-still-falsifiable: Task 3's atomicity test (a sequential-Put implementation leaves five rows and fails the empty-store assertion); Task 4's re-sort test (the fake returns BatchGet items reversed); Task 5's expired-run test (the fake evaluates `attribute_exists`); Task 14's `EXHAUSTIVENESS` test (an extra or missing apply branch changes the target list); Task 16's multi-message round trip (a divergence from the global `at` sort breaks the contiguity assertion); Task 19's `expect(h.seen).toHaveLength(0)` on the skip path and its `window: undefined` assertion on the wrapped-repo-throw test (a state the backstop tests cannot produce); Task 27's key-set assertion (the only layer that catches a wire-shape drift); Task 30's `noContent` test (the call id must appear under no-content AND be absent from the read-messages table).

**6. Impossibility-claim audit.** Every remaining claim that something is impossible, unreachable, unproducible or already-true, re-checked against the code this round:

| Claim | Verdict |
| --- | --- |
| `noContent` unproducible e2e | **WRONG, withdrawn.** `app/src/jobs/extraction.ts:131` returns `[]` on `transcript_status !== 'completed' OR !m.transcript`; the second disjunct fires for `''`. Chain verified through `dev.ts:427-441`, `voiceTranscripts.ts:89-92`, `messagesRepo.ts:1255-1260`. Test restored. |
| `age_30d` "needs backdated `created_at`, no seam offers it" | **WRONG REASON, right decision.** It is trivially true for seeded messages (`lean.ts:192-220` carry `ts`, not `created_at`), which is a seed defect, not the feature. Reason corrected; issue filed. |
| "the seed tenant already carries a `pets` value" | **WRONG, deleted.** `pets` appears in `lean.ts` only on units (`:151`, `:174`). Also unnecessary: `apply.ts:189-211` suggests into an empty field. |
| `no_new_client` unproducible e2e | **HOLDS.** `extractionRepo.ts:231` REMOVEs `_duePartition` and `dueAt` on claim; the four schedulers either supply gate-satisfying content (`sms`/`email`) or bypass the gate (`voice`/`triage`). |
| `ineligible_type` from a landlord inbound produces nothing | **HOLDS.** `webhooks/twilio.ts:1158-1161` arms only for `tenant_1to1` / `unknown_1to1`. |
| no e2e can reach a `failed` run | **HOLDS.** `extractionFake.extract` returns `{ ok: true }` on all three of its return paths, and no seam injects a driver or repo failure. |
| the backstop is unreachable from any site `processRow` wraps | **HOLDS**, and is now the basis of the fixed tests - `applyExtraction` and `driver.extract` are the unwrapped sites. |
| `address_no_usable_parts` (`apply.ts:226`) unreachable in production | **HOLDS.** `schema.ts:263-273` assigns `result.address` only when `Object.keys(parts).length > 0`. |
| `setVerdict`'s parent map always exists | **HOLDS by construction** - it is only ever called with a `(runId, target)` read off a suggestion row, which exists only because that run recorded a `suggested` decision. |
| `putRun`'s five items are far under the transaction limits | **HOLDS** (independently re-verified against the 100-item, duplicate-key, 400 KB and 4 MB limits). |

## SPEC CONCERNS

**The spec is FROZEN at v7.** Everything above is planned TO IT AS WRITTEN. The entries below are RECORDS, not requests: they do not ask for an amendment. Each is either a ruling I made where the spec is silent, or a deviation I am declaring openly so a reviewer inherits it rather than discovers it. Where one warrants follow-up it is filed as a `docs/issues/` entry in Task 31, not as a spec edit.

**1. A third `schema.ts` fold site the `empty_value_at_parse` amendment does not name.**
Spec 7.2 (amended) attributes the fold to `schema.ts:215-219` (scalars) and `:258-263` (address). There is a third: `schema.ts:236` folds `typeSuggestion.value` to absent unless it is exactly `'tenant'` or `'landlord'`. So an OFF-ENUM type value - non-empty, so not `empty_value_at_parse` - reaches Task 17's rule-4 UNEXPLAINED branch and records a REASONLESS drop plus an `unexplained dropped decision` warn. That is deliberately the loud behavior reviewers asked for rather than a guessed reason, and the case is near-unreachable in production (structured outputs constrain the enum; only the fake driver can produce it). Flagged because the first person to see that warn should know it is a known, named case and not a new defect.

**2. What `excluded` holds on a LIGHT window - ruled in-plan.**
Spec 6.3 (amended) enumerates what a light window carries but does not say what happens to `excluded`. A `char_budget` exclusion is only knowable after the budget fill, which requires the capping the amendment moved below the gate; an `age_30d` exclusion is free (a filter over rows already fetched). **Ruled: a light window carries `age_30d` exclusions only** (Task 16, `buildLightRunWindow`), and `RunWindow.detail` tells a reader which shape they are looking at, so an absent `char_budget` list is never misread as "nothing was budget-dropped".

**3. DECLARED DEVIATION: spec 11's e2e asserts `char_budget` and `noContent`, but not `age_30d`.**
`noContent` IS producible and IS asserted - an earlier revision of this plan wrongly declared it unproducible by reading only the first disjunct of `toUtterances`' guard; the second, `!m.transcript`, fires for an empty-string transcript, and the whole chain from the dev seam is verified in Task 30's producibility table. `age_30d` is the one deviation: producing it means using the SEEDED conversation, whose messages carry `ts` but no `created_at` (`app/src/lib/seed/lean.ts:192-220`) while production always stamps it (`app/src/repos/messagesRepo.ts:888`) - so the assertion would pin a seed defect, not the feature. Unit coverage is Task 16's `separates the two exclusion causes`. Filed as two `docs/issues/` entries in Task 31, one of them for the seed defect itself. **Not silently dropped, and not a request to change the spec.**

**4. DECLARED GAP: no e2e reaches a `failed` run, or a LIGHT window carrying message rows.**
The fake driver never returns `ok:false` and no seam injects a driver failure, so all five `error.kind`s are unit-only (Task 19). And a `no_new_client` skip - the only outcome that produces a populated light window - is unreachable end to end: `claim()` REMOVEs `_duePartition` and `dueAt` (`app/src/repos/extractionRepo.ts:231`), so a second tick finds zero due rows, and every scheduler either supplies content that satisfies the freshness gate (`sms`/`email`) or runs on a channel that bypasses it (`voice`/`triage`). Task 30's `empty_window` test covers `detail: 'light'` with an EMPTY message list, which is as close as the seams allow. Filed as `docs/issues/ai-run-log-no-e2e-light-window-rows.md` (Task 31).

**5. Resolved by amendments; recorded so no later round re-raises them.**
Three earlier concerns are settled and are NOT open: the bare `driver`/`model` naming collision with `messagingDriver` (now `aiExtraction*`-prefixed, Task 25); the cost of hoisting the full window computation above the freshness gate (now a light window, Task 16); and the `type` PATCH verdict, which v7 made value-dependent and Task 22 now implements with three tests including the reject case.

**6. Watch item, not a concern: the `runWindow.ts` <-> `jobs/extraction.ts` import cycle.**
`runWindow.ts` imports six constants from `jobs/extraction.ts`, which imports `buildLightRunWindow`/`buildFullRunWindow`. Task 16 Step 4 states the preferred preemptive fix (move the constants into `runTypes.ts` and re-export them from the job so both test files' imports are unchanged) and the trigger for doing it reactively.
